The completed immutable read-only snapshot now passes `audit_portfolio` after SQLite `quick_check(1)` and before receipt. ## v0.315.0 verified and flushed paper recovery snapshots

After SQLite online backup closes, the recovery boundary validates the same regular single-link destination, explicitly flushes its file content, fsyncs the parent directory on POSIX and opens the image with immutable read-only SQLite for `quick_check(1)`. Only the exact single `ok` result may reach a receipt. The receipt states whether directory-entry sync is supported; Windows reports false rather than implying a directory fsync. A verification timeout, malformed image, new SQLite sidecar, identity/metadata change or flush failure produces only fixed `PAPER_SNAPSHOT_*` review diagnostics and removes the failed candidate without altering retained snapshots.

## v0.312.0 offline replay-evidence contention and capacity boundary

The retained offline historical-replay evidence journal now applies an explicit ten-second SQLite connection and busy-timeout budget to schema access, cross-process writer admission, statements, final commit and replay. Only busy/locked exhaustion maps to `PAPER_REPLAY_JOURNAL_DATABASE_BUSY`; unrelated operational failures retain their prior behavior. ## v0.296.1 Windows replacement-regression portability

The three v0.296.0 replacement tests previously swapped a byte-identical clone from inside `_load`, while SQLite still held the original database open. POSIX permits that pathname replacement, but Windows correctly returned `WinError 5`; both hosted regression workflows therefore failed before reaching the production identity recheck.

The tests now create the same byte-identical clone and swap it from the instrumented `_connect` boundary after the implementation captures file identity but before the original SQLite connection opens. The next identity check must still fail closed. Production code, UI, permissions, emergency-stop behavior, limits and account checks are unchanged.

## v0.290.0 fee-composition store binding

`paper_provider_post_fee_reference_review` captures the exact provider-POST path/scope/digest and request-journal path/account digest before reconciliation. ## v0.230.0 exact provider POST/request-history composition

`paper_provider_post_review.review` accepts one explicit `PaperProviderPostBudget`, its exact current head and one to seven exact account-bound `BrokerRequestJournal` bindings. All journal paths must be distinct from each other and every SQLite companion/ownership path. Each request journal must retain the caller-pinned current head. The review replays complete tails, reads the POST history, samples request histories before/after prefix reconstruction and then rechecks every source.

Every completed non-interrupted POST attempt must find one prepared, not-yet-attempted request at its retained before head and one checked submission marker at its retained after head. Account, payload, hashed client ID, admission profile/scope and admission expected head must agree. Every current checked request marker must map back to one POST attempt. Missing accounts, pending or interrupted POST tails, stale/changing heads, legacy or extra markers, malformed/duplicate bindings and related paths retain `REVIEW_REQUIRED`; no repair or inferred transition is allowed.

The result exposes aggregate counts and fixed reasons only. It performs no writes, initialization, provider/vault access, admission, retry, recovery or resubmission. It does not bind raw provider bytes, discover another path/credential/process/host, attest source or broker-wide quota, reconcile fills/fees/holds/funds or grant routing/release permission. No UI or modifiable parameter changed; preserve seven tabs, black/bright-purple bottom-right help, OFF permissions, bounded limits, account checks, fail-closed exits and the independent emergency stop.

## v0.229.0 terminal original-ID POST-budget recovery

`paper_provider_post_recovery` must remain an explicit private-journal tool, never a background worker or resubmission path. ## v0.228.0 shared-local provider POST budget

`PaperProviderPostBudget` is a distinct private DELETE-mode SQLite hash chain, not the GET budget. Preserve exact expected-head admission, fixed provider scope, bounded events, single-link/path guards and one live OS ownership lease from reservation through result. The internal submission boundary must require a distinct budget path and record exactly one of `RESPONSE_OBSERVED`, `NOT_SENT` or `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`; the latter retains a full-day review block. An abandoned exact tail may be converted only by explicit confirmed interruption, preserving unknown outcome and never reclaiming, retrying or continuing automatically.

The provider reservation must precede the checked request-journal marker, and both must precede POST. A post-marker stop or credential/permission failure records `NOT_SENT` while the request remains permanently lookup-only. Any attempted POST followed by transport, parsing, observation or return failure records `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`. Never infer broker rejection, no fill, quota ownership, spendable funds or permission from a local budget result.

## v0.175.0 retained local request-event binding

Execution-quality review now replays the exact retained request journal and requires the selected original request's immutable `submission_admission` marker. ## v0.174.1 Windows-portable manifest identity

The bounded installed provider-result reader no longer treats cross-API `ctime` equality as file identity. Start with `ROADMAP.md`, the bundled read-only contract in `nexus/release_roadmap.py`, then `LAUNCH_DRIVE.md` and `VERSIONING.md`. The PORTAL exposes the same reference at `GET /api/roadmap`. Runtime users cannot edit it. Update source and regenerate ROADMAP.md together when milestones change.

Repository: S4-cyber/Nexus-Operative. Continue on `development/windows-portal-foundation`; verify the remote head and CI before writing. The local workspace can be an exported snapshot without .git. In that case use GitHub tree/commit/ref operations with the verified parent and an expected-head lease; do not overwrite concurrent work.

Prioritize v1.0 launch blockers in coherent batches; global opportunity expansion is secondary. Maintain broad refresh every 5 seconds, ranking every 15 seconds, top 30 AI candidates and adaptive acceleration as target settings. Individual Terrestrial/Solar/Universal permissions must affect buyer entry decisions; disengagement must not prevent risk-reducing exits. Pure decision throughput is not market fill throughput.

Current design is autonomous paper first. No external broker is connected. Alpaca paper is planned first, IBKR paper and Coinbase Advanced later. Respect existing explicit connection permissions. Preserve 0.x until all launch acceptance evidence exists and the owner approves 1.0; live-money activation is separately authorized.

Keep priced fills and linked paper-ledger writes atomic and idempotent. Existing traded accounts must not be merged or rewritten automatically. Never infer historical quantities from unpriced fills. A failed commit cannot acknowledge a fill. Validate restart, duplicate delivery, risk vetoes, partial cancellation and reconciliation.

Run appropriate tests plus `python -m compileall -q nexus tests`; validate the PORTAL script with `node --check` for UI edits. Windows packaging is verified by GitHub Actions, not a Linux unit-test result. Report actual test counts and CI status, exact commit and material remaining gates. Do not mark an entire milestone complete because a component passed.

Checkpoint policy: retain all commands and the latest two snapshots. A cold process validates checkpoint state by full prefix replay; warm restoration still hashes retained prefix payloads. Never bypass validation or silently prune command history to meet capacity limits. The disposable benchmark includes separate gateway-only and linked-ledger/tier-inclusive treatments. It excludes streaming, strategy queues, network and broker timing; do not claim complete market-path throughput from it. Its diagnostic settings use a context-local scope, never a process-wide environment override.

Logical archival requires halted, flat, reconciled execution accounting. It preserves every command, order ID, fill and cost assumption and leaves Nexus halted. Active order IDs are retired into retained history; they remain reserved and replayable forever within the configured retained-history bounds. Recovery enforces both the active-epoch limit and independent retained-history limits. Do not call this physical compaction or indefinite operation. The scripted operation drill is disposable fixture validation, not live-data autonomy.

The experimental forward runner is separate from the read-only observer. It requires explicit session start, active PORTAL, engaged tier and compatible linked accounting. It is BTC-only and always starts stopped after restart. Keep stop fencing, reserved exit capacity, freshness/identity checks and failure pauses. Modeled liquidity is assumed, not exchange depth. Its fixed momentum experiment is not a validated strategy; do not promote it to live authority or describe injected-quote tests as a sustained market run.

Forward session counters and heartbeats are diagnostic evidence, never the accounting source of truth. Interrupted/stale session records must not restore running permission. Session summaries are retained up to their explicit cap; do not delete them to admit new sessions. Only the fixed, fingerprinted paper experiment is admitted; research rankings/branch names and AI output cannot silently become runtime policy. Feed failures or long observation gaps must clear entry warmup.

Forward quote evidence retains only allowlisted public fields and explicitly labels injected transports versus the standard single-source public reader. Persist evidence before allowing a quote-driven fill. Retain the bounded evidence journal; cap exhaustion or head corruption pauses intake. Reports compare modeled fills to quote assumptions and session counters, but do not approve release or validate market performance. Active-session reviews are provisional across separate databases; stop for stable review. Receipt time is recorded after the default transport returns.

Forward runner admission also requires exclusive OS ownership of its local order-data directory. Hold ownership through any active fetch/cycle after stop; release on failure/stop completion and process exit. A leftover lock file is not permission to resume, and must never be deleted while active. This is local coordination, not a distributed lock or broker authorization.

Preserve wall/monotonic continuity checks before observation/submission/fill. Clock faults pause and clear warmup; submitted unfilled orders must still cancel. Exchange timestamp regressions clear warmup and count toward bounded failure pause. Custom-clock fixtures must explicitly supply a separate elapsed clock to test wall-time jumps.

The durable benchmark now also returns a separate temporary linked-ledger burst treatment. It measures serially drained bounded fixture queue residence and durable roundtrips, with duplicate replay, overflow/expiry and cold validation. Its market times are synthetic; it excludes actual feeds, strategy admission, streaming, network and broker execution, and cannot validate 30 market moves/second. Keep its scope distinct from the four original commit-return treatments.

DurableOrderGateway.execute_quote_order is an opt-in offline diagnostic operation, not used by the forward runner. It commits submit/terms/quote-fill/cancel together, retaining each ordinary replayable command. Preserve per-step linked ledger/tier/cap checks, rollback all new state on failure and acknowledge only after commit. No batch command type or deleted history is introduced. Keep atomic fixture timing separate from deployed runner or market latency claims.

Runtime quote execution must sample time and check stop/clock continuity after acquiring the SQLite write lock and restoring state. Persist the sampled evaluation time as an ordinary command argument, never a callable. Stale observations clear forward warmup; failed fills still cancel pending orders. Explicit synthetic evaluation times remain available to offline fixtures.

The forward_drill CLI exercises actual serial runner cycles in disposable child-process storage, retains repeated sessions and verifies fingerprints in a fresh process. Keep injected-source labels, 1–20 round bounds and no automatic production start. Its local full-cycle timings exclude real feeds, scheduling and broker IO; it cannot close sustained-market acceptance.

The default public reader rejects all HTTP redirects, including same-origin product changes and HTTPS downgrades. Require the exact requested response URL before reading a body; retain GET-only allowlisted paths, bounded reads/timeouts and successful-default-transport-only cache writes. These transport controls do not independently attest a market source.

Forward execution rechecks exchange timestamp age inside the post-lock clock hook, using the exact wall sample that passed monotonic continuity checks. Both BUY and risk-reducing SELL require fresh observations. Retained evidence independently compares exchange age with the actual journaled fill evaluation time; disagreement must remain a review blocker. Do not change the fixed policy fingerprint merely to relax freshness.

Any expected storage/integrity error during forward order cancellation is a fatal cleanup failure, not an ordinary strategy veto. Stop intake immediately, retain committed fills, release ownership after the cycle and record a PAUSED review marker when storage permits. Lost cancellation acknowledgment is not proof of an open order; inspect the durable ledger and retry cancellation idempotently. Never erase the original session review blocker after manual cleanup.

Forward recovery GET reports pending remainders, retained positions and bounded recent-session review reasons. It never starts, cancels, initializes an account or repairs evidence. PORTAL inspect is read-only; cancellation is an explicit action through the existing paper cancel API. Disable that control during this runner's active cycle and recheck before posting; snapshots do not reserve admission or prove absence of another process. Keep historical cleanup markers visible after successful manual cancellation.

Give parametrized large-body fixtures explicit short IDs: pytest exports node IDs through PYTEST_CURRENT_TEST and Windows rejects environment values longer than 32,767 characters. Keep the full payload assertion; do not shrink coverage to hide a collection/setup failure.

PORTAL beginner help is a standing user requirement: read UI_STANDARDS.md. Every button and editable parameter needs practical data-help text. Keep the bottom-right panel black with bright purple high-contrast text; hide on pointer leave/focus loss and support Escape. Use helpButton(description) for all dynamic buttons; never display entered values or secrets. Run control-help coverage/behavior tests and full-script syntax checks for new controls.

Browser help acceptance uses intercepted fixtures only via tools/portal_browser_smoke.py and the optional ui dependency group. Windows safety CI retains screenshot/report artifacts. A browser/install failure is not a visual pass. Preserve help during keyboard focus auto-scroll while the control remains visible; hover-off still hides immediately. Native-window/profile acceptance remains separate.

Alpaca paper foundation is disconnected: read ALPACA_PAPER_CONTRACT.md. Previews and bounded observation diagnostics are not an adapter, entitlement, fee reconciliation or durable broker recovery. Never feed cumulative quantities/average prices into the synthetic USD-fee ledger, infer no fill from a timeout, or treat pending_cancel as final. Keep gateway external submission denied until separately authorized integration and acceptance exist.

The real Windows browser run exposed automatic-scroll hover loss. Keep help visible while its control is actually hovered and visible, including scroll events queued after pointer entry; hide immediately on pointer leave. Failure artifacts include the current control, event history and screenshot to distinguish implementation defects from tool failures.

Broker fee projection is pure supplied-data aggregate arithmetic, not a raw activity adapter or reconciled broker account. Require explicit execution prices/IDs and execution-linked fee activity records; never infer fills from cumulative averages or missing fees as zero. Keep BTC/USD fee units separate, reject conflicting duplicate IDs and flag unverified coverage. Even matching fixtures never grant routing, attest a broker account or write the synthetic ledger.

Pointer event targets may have no matching control even when no help is active. Require a non-null control before comparing identity or calling contains; retain background pointerout regression coverage.

Account Matrix continuity: read MATRIX_ACCOUNT.md. Singularity uses whole observed paper liquidation estimates; Fractal uses per-holding unrealized USD P/L. Never substitute public ticker movement, decorative rain or synthetic events for account returns. Sampling writes only its separate bounded observation store, requires active PORTAL and discards mixed-account snapshots; missing marks create gaps. Keep adjustment inspection read-only and preserve beginner help. Retained month/year range selection is not a full-year data guarantee.

Global market expansion and computer-off cloud operation are explicit user goals. Read CLOUD_OPERATION.md for free-tier/no-dedicated-account preferences and reviewed provider limits. No anonymous always-on host has been selected; do not silently enroll, spend, enable paid upgrades or expose the local development API. Existing OS ownership is local only; cloud needs authenticated control, durable state, distributed fencing, encryption/key management and demonstrated computer-off paper acceptance.

Matrix observations now move completed UTC days from the capped active table into retained history within one FULL-synchronous SQLite transaction. Keep original IDs/timestamps/payloads and the highest active ID; a failed move must roll back both tables. Total retained admission is independent of the active cap. GET must read legacy databases without schema writes and combine archived and active rows into one bounded actual-point response. This is logical retention in one growing database, not physical offload, extrema-preserving rollups, unlimited operation or encrypted cloud storage.

Before appending Matrix observations, verify the active highest ID is above the retained highest ID. Missing/conflicting active history must block intake before quote IO rather than reuse a retained identity or repair evidence silently.

Matrix storage admission has independent sample, byte and free-disk limits. Keep the 1 GiB SQLite page limit, 256 MiB reserve plus journal/write headroom, pre-quote archive capacity estimate, post-fetch recheck and 64 KiB new-record bound. A failed post-fetch check or SQLite allocation failure cannot acknowledge a sample or leave a half-moved archive. Preserve all history, close connections and show practical collection warnings; these controls neither stop nor authorize order execution and are not a cloud quota or reserved disk allocation.

Collective hub continuity: read MATRIX_HUB.md. Canonical public OHLCV identities include venue/product/interval/start; numerical equivalents deduplicate, conflicting content is quarantined without overwriting accepted history. All actual hub/model/council code remains local, with no cloud, external AI provider or order authority. Respect fixed byte/row/cache caps; do not silently evict evidence or treat hashes/read-only file attributes as signatures or encryption. Historical first receipt is not point-in-time source attestation.

Hub momentum is a trained linear baseline with 5/20/60-bar features and 1/3/12-bar targets, training-only scaling and purged chronological holdout. Never select/tune on held-out results or present MAE/directional hits as profit. Dataset generations increment on new records/conflicts, not duplicates; publication rechecks the generation and signals abstain after changes or expiry. Model artifacts persist and reuse unchanged dataset identities. The legacy external-adapter diagnostic remains fixtures; hub preparation now embeds three actual ridge feature specialists plus validation/provenance rule reviewers. Read MATRIX_HUB.md for engine-version refresh, fixed three-block checks, retained candidate artifacts and optional future local-weight upgrades. An explicit bounded local refresh worker is available from v0.83; no LLM runtime is installed; no model output may silently replace the forward runner's fixed policy or gain execution permission. New hub controls require beginner help and the hub-controls Node check.

Workspace/startup continuity: read STARTUP_GUIDE.md. Keep seven named tabs, keyboard/ARIA navigation, preserved form/session state and global pause/halt controls. To Do checkboxes are read-only observations, never manual completion or execution gates. GET /api/onboarding/tasks must not initialize accounts/databases, train, call external services, expose secrets or auto-connect. Missing tables mean setup needed; corrupt/unreadable state is unknown. Clear stale checks on refresh failure. Bank/payment/broker linking remain unavailable, external AI optional, built-in numerical models included and cloud planned. Preserve meaningful beginner help for dynamic disabled checkboxes and task jump buttons; run workspace_tabs_smoke.cjs and update browser traversal for all panels.

Hub validation v0.82: engine v2 includes fixed expanding-window refits and delayed-fill cost diagnostics; see MATRIX_HUB.md. Share causal features without fitting scalers on future data. Keep all feature views and 10/25/50 bps cost cases, explicit no-trade/numeric-abstention states, unit-capital scope, fixed 30 bps threshold and one-full-bar pre-entry delay. Council review requires at least five trades and positive modeled net return in each distinct 25 bps fold, plus existing MAE checks; this is still no execution permission or evidence of future profitability. No winner selection/tuning or auto-promotion. Build/prepare use the same local job lock and release on errors. v0.83 adds the bounded opt-in refresh worker described in MATRIX_HUB.md; sustained unattended collection/acceptance remains open.

Engine/throttle semantics (v0.85, explicit user requirement): Portal is the research engine; its explicit start also starts bounded local learning with selected market/interval/window and public history selected by default. Public checkbox/UI/Portal selection is active by default; checkbox/navigation/status alone never starts work. Existing standalone refresh API/core omitted flags remain saved-only for compatibility; Portal passes its explicit default true. Repeated engine-on requests are idempotent and do not re-arm budgets. Engine off stops research and paper. Emergency Stop is a trading throttle cut: durable halt/cancel plus runner stop, leaving observer/learning/account observation active. Reset preserves research on/off state and never engages paper. A newer cut must win over concurrent reset or Engage. Keep the emergency button usable during pending engagement and after an unconfirmed cut; never hide it in a tab.

Engage Paper explicitly starts only the fixed BTC paper experiment with confirmed virtual funds and existing tier/account gates. Research models remain advisory; deposited real funds/broker execution remain unsupported. Serialize trading admission/reset/stop, publish local cut/pause latches before waiting, recheck admission after preparation and preserve durable stop fences. Test actual accounting, retained partial fills, in-flight fetch, start/reset races, rollback and the actual engine controller.

Local refresh invariants: starts stopped after process restart; explicit Portal start or manual active-engine arm, captured market/interval/240–1000 bar scope, 60-second polls, 60-poll/eight-training-attempt caps including failed generation-race fits. Shared manual/worker job admission and separate local OS worker ownership; never release ownership during an active cycle. Stop/engine-off/shutdown fence publication after acknowledgment but do not preempt imports/calculation/sealing. Trading cuts do not revoke research admission. Retain candidates/old packs under quotas. Callback guards are internal, not order permission or persisted callables. Missing data waits; faults pause with sanitized reasons; clock/root/admission changes stop. Always clean up refresh even if other shutdown cleanup fails. Read-only status/To Do never arm. Run hub_refresh_smoke.cjs, engine_controls_smoke.cjs, lifecycle/OS-death/stop-race and Windows checks.

Public collection invariants: fixed Coinbase public paths, no redirects/response-URL changes, bounded reads/timeouts and closed-window filtering. Bootstrap up to four 150-bar pages, then one only after a new closed interval plus five-second settling; at most 12 request admissions/session including failures. Preflight conservative storage/artifact/disk headroom before each request; receipt-time clock/root/stop/admission checks before atomic ingestion and continuity before publication. An admitted request can finish after research stop, but cannot ingest after acknowledgment. Preserve earlier pages/revisions/models on failure; quarantine conflicts and pause. Source receipt/URL matching is not independent provenance or execution authority. Keep explicit saved-only requests network silent; use injected data in regression tests.


v0.86 product roadmap: nexus/release_roadmap.py now includes nine product delivery stages alongside the nine technical evidence tracks. Keep secure provider-held funding/available cash, connected broker paper, governed live autonomy, computer-off/shared-hub acceptance and final 1.0 explicitly visible in source, generated ROADMAP.md and PORTAL. Stage status is never runtime permission. Funding remains preview-only; 1.0 requires all agreed production workflows accepted and owner release approval, with separate live activation. Never imply deposits, custody, brokerage access or cloud deployment already exist.

Public quote efficiency: read PUBLIC_DATA_EFFICIENCY.md. Runtime default ticker requests share at most one pending request per allowlisted product, with bounded follower waits, original receipt timestamps and fresh-at-return validation. Keep HTTP allowlist/redirect/body checks unchanged, wall/monotonic cache and transport guards, copied results and no stale fallback. Injected transports never use/write the production cache; explicit fixture clocks never join runtime flights or metrics. Every leader exit must signal waiters and release its slot. Do not cancel research readers because the paper runner stopped; execution still checks its own stop/clock/freshness gates. Diagnostics GET is process-local, no-store and zero IO; it never authorizes orders. Keep fixed-shape counters, no raw quotes/errors/secrets and no auto metrics reset. Use fixture concurrency, failure/timeout, clock, exchange-age, reader-copy and actual runner stop tests. Run feed_efficiency_smoke.cjs for diagnostic and product-roadmap rendering, plus existing six controllers and browser acceptance. Shared-request timings are not end-to-end execution speed or profitability evidence.


v0.87 emergency scheduling: read EMERGENCY_CONTROL.md. POST halt is async, publishes its local latch/epoch before waiting and submits/coalesces one cleanup job on a lazy dedicated single-worker executor, independent of both AnyIO feed workers and the asyncio general executor. Never acknowledge success until durable halt/cancellation, runner stop fencing and audit return. Shield the underlying job from caller cancellation; complete pending/reset fencing from the concurrent Future callback, not HTTP lifetime. Do not queue repeated cuts. Failed submit can enqueue before thread creation fails: cancel abandoned work and retire the failed executor. Lifespan drains the lane before other cleanup and rejects new job admission during drain while keeping local intake latched. No live/broker authority is added.

GET status is async/no-store/zero IO; retain synchronous status() as the snapshot helper. Expose CLEAR/PENDING/VERIFIED/UNCONFIRMED without treating a local request as verified completion. Reset captures its epoch and rejects pending cuts before general-worker scheduling, then checks again under trading control and after durable reset. A reset queued before a newer cut must never clear that cut. Reset still does not engage or change research on/off state. Keep Emergency Stop usable during pending Engage and after failures; pending reset is disabled in UI. Tests must exercise the actual ASGI app with saturated AnyIO and asyncio general workers, caller cancellation, coalesced requests, startup/submit failure including actual thread-start failure, shutdown and queued-reset races. Scheduling isolation is not a hard realtime or storage/network deadline. Run the full regression suite and all seven controllers; preserve existing execution/ownership/clock fences.


v0.88 runner cut signal: Emergency admission sets runner.request_cut before durable-worker/lane admission, without acquiring runner control/execution locks or doing IO. The runner checks it at preparation/cycle/submit/fetch boundaries and the post-SQLite-write-lock execution clock hook. Never clear it in prepare/start/stop/engine retry; only the reset coordinator clears after durable reset plus final epoch validation under _cut_lock. Keep _stop separate to preserve STOP records and ownership lifetime; cut-only thread exit must not release ownership before fenced cleanup. enabled=false can mean intake blocked while cleanup/ownership remains pending. Protect previous session identity if recovery is interrupted before a new START. Preserve all accounting, policy and clock hooks.

Run tests/test_emergency_storage.py with actual order/portfolio SQLite locks, BUY and SELL post-lock vetoes, busy timeout/retry, missing control worker and before/after-commit process death plus fresh-process validation. The immediate pre-write signal is volatile: never infer a committed database halt after restart or auto-resume/auto-repair pending orders. v0.89 adds the independent intent recovery described below. Concurrent starts blocked by the cut use HTTP 409; do not weaken entry/account checks. Update EMERGENCY_CONTROL and the roadmap/handoff with exact test/CI evidence.

Independent cut intent (v0.89): read EMERGENCY_CONTROL.md. The dedicated worker persists a bounded local presence latch beside the order database before SQLite halt, under trading control. Startup presence/unreadable state blocks intake and sets the runner Event; a clear database plus intent is UNCONFIRMED, never auto-repaired. Durable validated halt can recover VERIFIED. Reset stops/commits reset/removes intent before final epoch clearance; removal failure remains locally UNCONFIRMED and newer cut wins. Attempt gateway halt/stop even if intent persistence fails. Never perform intent IO on async admission/status or claim persistence before the worker writes; worker/trading-lock pre-write crash window and Windows directory-sync limitations remain. Do not follow malformed/symlink intent contents or silently delete a directory. Preserve paper-only authority and explicit reset/retry.

v0.90 broker request recovery: read ALPACA_PAPER_CONTRACT.md. BrokerRequestJournal is explicit-path, caller-paper-account-scoped and disconnected. Persist canonical original-ID terms, one uncertain-attempt boundary and allowlisted observations with FULL commit before acknowledgment. Replay every retained event under SQLite write serialization; no request/event eviction, automatic retry, broker lookup/send, synthetic ledger application or authenticated reconciliation. Current duplicates append nothing; conflict/stale/UUID reuse rejects without state changes. Caps are 128 requests/4096 events/4096 bytes per event/64 MiB database plus tracker snapshot limits. Read-only recovery never creates missing state; damaged/foreign state requires review, not silent initialization. Hash chains do not attest a broker or detect valid-tail deletion. Fixture API/button use temporary storage only; run tests/test_broker_request_journal.py, tools/broker_request_smoke.cjs and actual intercepted browser traversal. Preserve original-ID uncertainty across restart and lost acknowledgments; future transport/retry remains separately authorized work.

v0.91 lookup recovery: broker_lookup_policy is pure supplied-result classification; begin_lookup/finish_lookup only record disconnected diagnostics, never send GET/POST, retry an order or run a timer. Retain four total lookup attempts/request, one pending ticket/journal, shared cooldowns, completion-slot reservation and ordinary-write fencing while pending. Replay outcome+accepted observation atomically; wrong/stale/corrupt results leave pending state unchanged. Timeout/404 never clear submission uncertainty; auth/unsupported responses block lookup for review. Retry-After accepts bounded seconds/HTTP dates, never truncates a long wait to permit early retry. Exact completed-event replay cannot clear a newer pending ticket. Preserve all original-ID/account/quantity/fee boundaries and no automatic resume/resubmission. Explicit caller wall time is not trusted monotonic timing; separate journals/remote requests are not coordinated. Old v0.90 readers reject new event kinds. Run tests/test_broker_lookup.py and the existing request/controller/browser acceptance. No authenticated transport or credentials are installed.

v0.92 cash path: broker_cash_path audits supplied execution history with all declared linked fees reserved at execution, including later postings. Share the aggregate projection's validated normalized records; preserve its public reconcile output and no account/ledger/network IO. Positive ending balances never repair earlier USD/BTC deficits. Equal UTC microsecond timestamps require prefunding all debits; never sort IDs to infer execution order or use another same-group receipt as funding. Nonterminal orders require reservation review; invalid/incomplete aggregate evidence prevents path evaluation. Minima are opening/group-end only; retain a capped sample list plus full violation count. All broker/spendable-funds/routing flags stay false. This retrospective audit must never leak later-known fees into earlier strategy/model decisions. The existing fee fixture button/API are offline, no-store, guarded and sanitized; run tests/test_broker_cash_path.py, original projection tests and tools/broker_request_smoke.cjs plus browser help. Secure funding/account reconciliation remains unimplemented acceptance.

v0.93 interrupted lookup review: pending_lookup() is copied read-only current-ticket/head inspection with no missing-state initialization. interrupt_lookup requires exact account/client/ticket/current head, explicit bool confirmation and nonregressing supplied clock; compare references inside SQLite write admission. Retain LOOKUP_INTERRUPTED, verify expected_head equals the retained predecessor on replay, acknowledge after FULL commit only. Preserve original submission uncertainty, partial observations, spent attempt budget, existing waits/review blocks and add conservative cooldown. Late completion for the interrupted ticket cannot apply. Exact historical interruption replay appends nothing and cannot clear a newer pending ticket. This is declared local review, not transport cancellation, proof of stopped process, broker absence or resubmit permission. Never auto-interrupt/expire/resume or expose a general write endpoint. Old v0.92 readers reject new events; copied/restored journals do not grant broker ownership. Run tests/test_broker_lookup_interruption.py plus lookup/request/controller/browser checks; fixture UI remains temporary, no actual account tickets.

v0.94 paper inspection: read ALPACA_PAPER_CONTRACT.md and nexus/alpaca_paper_inspection.py. Require strict per-call paper-read confirmation before IO, bounded credentials/responses/lists, no proxies/redirects, exact URL checks, stable expected account identity, wall/monotonic/permission fences, nonblocking local admission, decimal parsing and sanitized failures. No retries, order methods or persistence. Missing/unknown account permission/funds fields, pending transfers/fees, unsupported holdings and open-order reservations require review. Never infer qty_available from qty or use leveraged buying power. Reported cash/BTC ceilings never grant spendable funds, reconciled account or routing authority; sequential reads are not atomic. v0.95 explicit paper read onboarding: nexus/paper_credentials.py and nexus/paper_onboarding.py now wire Windows-only isolated pair save/status/removal and separately confirmed one-time inspection into Accounts. Strict confirmations, one bounded versioned OS record separate from AI, exact checked WinVault backend, verified save/delete readback, local mutation epochs before IO, nonblocking admission, stale-result rejection and final entry recheck are required. Failed/superseded mutation remains locally blocked; this is volatile local fencing, not remote cancellation, distributed ownership or durable key revocation. Four fixed POST routes require loopback peer/local hostname/exact Origin before bounded duplicate-free JSON parsing; never expose secret validation inputs or raw errors. GUI clears saved password fields, keeps removal available during reads and suppresses old responses. Scope is application read policy, not provider-issued key permissions. To Do's optional row opens no vault and stays unticked. Tests inject both OS and HTTP; never retrieve actual keys/contact brokers during development checks. v0.96 journal-bound lookup: read ALPACA_PAPER_CONTRACT.md. broker_paper_recovery.recover_original and paper_credentials.recover_saved are internal explicitly confirmed operations on an existing uncertain original request and required expected account UUID; no new runtime/UI/CLI/default-path invocation exists. Commit LOOKUP_BEGIN before account/order IO. Share the fixed-path bounded GET session with manual inspection. Checked journal hooks resample session permission/time after SQLite write admission/full replay and before commit, never persist callbacks; post-commit veto can lose acknowledgment but must retain committed history. Saved-key checks are sampled local fencing, not transport cancellation/distributed ownership. Journal event/schema compatibility remains, with no inherent transport provenance/attestation. v0.97 REST activities: alpaca_activity_normalizer is pure supplied Trading REST v2 BTC/USD FILL/CFEE/FEE normalization, not a fetcher or reconciled account. Read ALPACA_PAPER_CONTRACT.md. Require explicit qty/price/time/cumulative/remaining values and supplied order links, preserve nanoseconds/source REST identities, deduplicate semantic equivalents and reject conflicting IDs, unsupported/mixed/corrected/unmodeled financial records. Keep combined 2 MiB/2,000 activity/1,000 order/64-field/4 KiB-value bounds and no private extras in output. CFEE BTC and FEE USD debits must preserve signs/precision; no fee descriptions, nearest-time/amount matching, order hints or date fields may become guessed execution links/posting instants. v0.98 activity collection: alpaca_activity_collection.collect_activities and paper_credentials.collect_saved are internal explicitly confirmed one-shot reads with required account/order/window scope, no runtime/UI/CLI/default window/cursor or automatic continuation. Read ALPACA_PAPER_CONTRACT.md. Use exact canonical fixed-host creation-window queries (UTC whole seconds, <=one closed day), max two 100-row pages and four GETs including stable account reads. Freeze/validate caller orders, normalize accumulated pages before further IO, preserve opaque source cursor spelling while comparing canonical identities, and reject loops/conflicting/unknown/malformed data. Never equate a short/empty page, a creation filter or scope hash with complete/attested activity/fee coverage or a resume permission. Late faults/key changes discard the invocation; no partial success, storage, cache, source receipt or order-journal mutation. Shared local reader/time/key/URL/body/redirect fences remain; collection-wide durable pacing/retention/provenance and actual-account acceptance are open. v0.99 activity retention: read ALPACA_PAPER_CONTRACT.md. BrokerActivityStore uses explicit paths/account UUIDs only; retain canonical allowlisted activities/order snapshots/window references with FULL atomic commit and complete bounded replay. Optional collector/collect_saved activity_store remains internal and explicitly supplied, never a default/API/autorun. Inspect missing state without initialization; reject existing empty/foreign/corrupt/symlink/capped history without repair/eviction. Deduplicate exact receipts and globally reuse matching activity IDs; reject conflicting activities and broker/client/side bindings atomically. Recheck session/key admission under write serialization and before commit, then after commit; a lost return or post-commit revocation may retain a receipt, never infer rollback/retry/permission. No page/cursor/source provenance is retained: always SUPPLIED_UNATTESTED and all coverage/fee/funds/account/ledger/routing/live/resume authority false. Hashes are neither attestations nor valid-tail-deletion protection. Preserve date-only unlinked fees and separate request/synthetic ledgers. v0.100 release trials: read RELEASE_ACCEPTANCE.md. `python -m nexus.release_trials --output PATH` runs fixed bounded disposable child profiles (20 forward sessions/280 cycles, 24 operation rounds, backup/restore, four 30-cycle capacity treatments plus bursts, real loopback HTTP/lifespan). No default/background/runtime trial registration or actual provider/credential access. HTTP child denies/counts external connects and keyring access; scripted other profiles are injected fixtures, not a claimed network sandbox. Windows safety CI retains source-fingerprinted JSON; new-source pending runs are not passes. v0.101 retained activity review: read ALPACA_PAPER_CONTRACT.md. BrokerActivityStore.review uses one full replay-validated read-only snapshot and broker_activity_review to group/deduplicate reported order snapshots, detect equal-time conflicts and quantity/terminal/unchanged-quantity-average revisions, and normalize all unique explicit retained activities against latest reported-time diagnostic snapshots only when unambiguous. Never choose equal-time ties by arrival/ID, erase historical conflicts, infer fills from averages, link fee hints or create fee posting instants. A normalizer failure returns no partial normalization; >1,000 distinct orders raises rather than truncates. Declared creation-window unions/gaps describe caller request intent only, not received coverage/boundary semantics or provenance. Always REVIEW_REQUIRED and all authority/coverage/funds/ledger/resume flags false. No new API/UI/default path/autorun. The release trial suite now has six profiles, adding a two-batch temporary supplied-activity merge; preserve all prior profiles/limits and record a new source-fingerprinted report after source changes. Run test_broker_activity_review, storage/normalizer/collection/release tests, full regression and nine controllers. Actual-account/pacing/fee/hold/source acceptance stays open before provider-paper routing.

v0.101.1 Windows regression correction: passive provider_research.status must never call enabled/retrieve/configured or open the AI OS vault. credential_vault.local_status reads one immutable process-local hint without waiting behind OS operations; it is stale/unverified metadata, not credential or provider authority. Restart/failure is NOT_CHECKED, not proof of absence. Only explicit install/removal/retrieval/configured/request operations update it; actual requests still re-read credentials and cannot use cached presence as permission. Do not confuse this AI hint with the independent Alpaca paper entry/status controls. To Do/readiness/status remain vault/network silent and show unknown/stale labels. The HTTP release profile injects the Windows-only policy branch on every OS with keyring denied and exercises readiness/provider status/To Do, but this is not a native Windows pass. Run test_passive_ai_status, vault/provider/onboarding/trial/connector/roadmap tests, full regression, ten Node controllers (new ai_status_smoke.cjs), browser fixture and fresh source-fingerprinted trials. ROADMAP.md/API now include a progress overview/diagram; version/test count is never completion percentage. Investigate exact-SHA CI failures before proceeding; v0.100 Windows regression exposed the passive AI-vault access, inherited by v0.101 until this fix. No real keys/provider calls in development.

v0.101.2 optional-AI credential hardening: ai_onboarding now registers the original connect/disconnect URLs using local peer/Host/exact Origin checks before bounded duplicate-free secret parsing. Do not reintroduce Pydantic secret inputs/echoes or raw backend exceptions. Explicit OS operations run in to_thread; passive status remains cache-only/no-store/nonblocking. credential_vault binds the exact checked built-in WinVaultKeyring object, validates bounded printable ASCII keys, verifies save/readback and removal/absence, treats absent removal idempotently, rejects malformed stored keys and leaves hints unknown on unconfirmed/lost acknowledgments. No automatic rollback/delete/retry or actual credential/provider tests. Local removal neither revokes provider keys nor changes environment config/cancels in-flight requests; keep UI help/confirmation honest. Run test_ai_onboarding plus vault/passive/provider/release tests, full suite/ten controllers, intercepted help and fresh trial evidence. Tests cover malformed/cross-origin/oversized/duplicate bodies, readback failure, backend substitution, lost acknowledgments and event-loop responsiveness during injected slow OS work. Native Windows profile/ACL/key lifecycle and independent security review remain open before release.

Require same expected account and exact type; commit BEGIN before any GET, one pending session across cooperating processes, <=four GETs and fixed 60-second recorded-result cooldown with bounded Retry-After extension/auth/review blocks. Full bounded canonical/hash replay, FULL commits, post-lock/precommit clock/permission samples, completion-slot reservation, no eviction/repair/automatic retry/expiry/clear. v0.103 owned paper-read interruption: read ALPACA_PAPER_CONTRACT.md. Optional paced _ReadSession holds its local OS lock from before BEGIN through all IO/result/context cleanup. Retain hash of canonical journal path and lock device/inode; validate ownership at session checkpoints. Never unlink a lock file, auto-expire/resume, clear completed auth/review blocks, infer no request or change independent order tickets/accounting. Internal interrupt requires explicit confirmation, exact expected account/current head/pending token, matching owned-BEGIN identity and acquired nonblocking same-file OS ownership; legacy/unowned/moved/copied/replaced-lock history remains blocked. Append INTERRUPTED_UNKNOWN, preserve counts/history and at least a full-day cooldown from review; duplicate acknowledgment only while that interruption is latest. Admission callbacks resample after write-lock replay and precommit; faults/death/lost acknowledgments preserve pending/committed evidence. ## v0.119 local HTTP paper-read recovery acceptance

The existing fourteen-profile offline release suite now exercises the Accounts paced-read workflow over real loopback TCP/HTTP, with an in-memory fake vault, injected paper responses and a disposable journal. It verifies missing-status reads create no storage, confirmation/origin/scope rejection, a separately saved fixture pair, one four-GET inspection and validated inventory download data, cooldown denial before another transport, and credential removal completing while an injected read is blocked. The revoked read must return only the fixed unavailable error; it cannot acknowledge an account observation. Its uncertain pending reservation must remain. Status then runs with the original denied vault restored, and a fresh child process replays the same pending/cooldown state without any credential/network access or journal-byte changes.

The release validator now requires every nested paper-HTTP check, the exact five injected GETs (four successful-session requests plus one blocked/revoked request), explicit fixture scope and false provider/vault/financial/release flags. A missing check, changed count, promoted authority or broker relabeling cannot pass saved-evidence validation. Generate the current source-version report before the full suite, since current report fixtures exercise the evolved HTTP profile contract. Existing older reports remain historical evidence for their exact old source, not current acceptance.

These are source-process integration and persistence tests, not actual Windows Credential Manager/ACL/profile acceptance, real broker requests, broker-wide quota coordination, source/fee/hold/funds reconciliation, sustained market performance or release approval. There is no new runtime endpoint, automatic read/retry/resume, key onboarding or order/funding/cloud activation. Windows/browser/installer evidence must still match the exact new source; all independent and owner 1.0 gates remain open.

## v0.120 private journal file preflight

A disposable reproduction showed that opening a WAL-mode pacing database for status could create new -wal/-shm files. Pacing, supplied broker-activity retention and disconnected broker-request journals now share a bounded file preflight before SQLite opens. Reject nonregular files, leaf symlinks, multiple hard links, oversized files, sampled path/descriptor identity changes, unsupported SQLite read/write-version header bytes and any existing WAL/SHM sidecar, including an orphan beside a missing main file. Read at most20 unbuffered header bytes; recheck newly created files before SQLite initialization. Existing file caps, schema/account/hash replay, atomic DELETE-mode writes and rollback-journal recovery are retained. Existing invalid/empty databases still require ordinary schema/binding review; no guard initializes or repairs them.

Unsupported layouts fail for both reads and writes without converting journal mode, deleting sidecars or silently creating a replacement main database. Keep the entire private file set intact while stopped and seek a reviewed recovery plan; do not erase or force-convert files to bypass a block. These sampled local checks are not an atomic SQLite descriptor binding, protection against every concurrent path replacement, an ACL audit, encryption or source/financial attestation. Other application stores are outside this specific change; no universal filesystem guarantee is claimed.

The existing pacing release profile now requires actual WAL read/write denials and unchanged files. New cross-store tests reject FIFO/directory/symlink/hardlink paths before SQLite, retain orphan sidecars, cover descriptor replacement/growth and preserve normal DELETE-mode replay/duplicate behavior. Accounts pacing-status failures now correctly describe local-history review and explicitly say the check reads no keys and sends no broker request; preserve its files rather than changing credential setup automatically. No new runtime operation, retry, reset, broker routing or authority is added. Generate the current-version report before the full suite; all14 profiles and saved-evidence checks remain required. Actual provider/profile/account/fee/hold, sustained market, funding/live/cloud and independent/owner release gates stay open.

## v0.131 declared collection-window barrier

The private accepted-reconciliation composition now validates that the union of **accepted caller-declared creation windows** covers the entire envelope from the opening observation's first read to the closing observation's last read, before any balance arithmetic. Earlier synthetic reconciliation fixtures used observation dates outside their retained requested windows; they now use dates within those windows and explicitly prove the old out-of-window case is rejected. Only receipts referenced by replayed acceptance records contribute. Missing/changed receipts, a changed retained head or scope count, reversed/overlapping observation windows, outside endpoints and interior gaps fail closed. Merge adjacent/overlapping/duplicate intervals without bridging a positive gap; use canonical UTC nanoseconds, never float rounding. Bind the window-union digest in the v2 reconciliation input scope and retain the final three-journal recheck.

This is geometric coverage of requested filters, **not received activity coverage**. Exact endpoint equality does not verify a provider's inclusive/exclusive filter semantics; `requested_window_boundaries_verified`, source/activity/fee/hold/account/funds/routing/live/release flags remain false. Short final pages still only establish bounded cursor completion. Late/corrected executions, fees and sequential balance uncertainty remain open independent reviews. No provider/vault call, API/UI/control, automatic retry/continuation, evidence write or ledger/order/funding/cloud authority was added. Keep all actual observations, accepted journals and private diagnostic output outside Git; the existing standalone-report artifact guard applies to the v2 policy too.

The sixteenth release profile now requires out-of-window rejection, covered accepted geometry and false provider-boundary authority in addition to all prior acceptance/replay fences. Generate the current-version16-profile report before full tests, then verify saved evidence against the exact source. Regression checks include pre-arithmetic rejection with unchanged files, contiguous/overlapping/duplicate union, a one-second interior gap, exact endpoints and a one-nanosecond overrun. The general supplied observation-review tool retains its explicitly conditional diagnostic scope; this stricter barrier belongs to accepted reconciliation.

v0.132 Matrix observation standard (owner clarification): Singularity is one whole-account movement graph. Fractal is a coordinated multi-point view of all observed holdings, with individually traceable position histories. The implemented shared-time constellation encodes signed unrealized USD P/L around a zero ring on a shared range scale, keeps measured history below and removes missing/closed latest nodes. Angles and connecting edges are layout, never correlation, forecast or execution activity. Preserve gaps, downsampled dots, accessible quantities/value summaries and practical black/purple control help. Mode switching and adjustment inspection remain IO-free. Current data is local paper observations, not connected broker coverage. Future position sources must preserve the same comprehensive distinction and honest source/timing labels.

No engine, order, account, feed, vault, provider or paid AI operation starts from this action. Existing settings files (including empty ones), retained accounting/forward journals or sidecars and emergency cut intent suppress automatic risk selection; existing explicit OFF choices and custom settings survive reload. Initialization failures roll back and never grant a running session. Explicit first tier choices made before settings loading also win. The low-level missing-switch fallback for existing installations remains OFF; this supersedes the historical blanket all-tiers-OFF new-install wording.

The ordinary fresh path is open EXE → Start Portal → separately confirm Engage Paper. Public-history collection, BTC hourly/1,000-bar research and built-in numerical learners already have defaults. Solar/Universal do not add engine processing power; no external AI/payment/provider account is needed for the local paper experiment. Compatible unused virtual accounting is already created and linked on confirmed Engage; do not demand a separate Initialize or Save Allocations step. Existing traded/incompatible accounting still requires review, never automatic reset/migration. Restart remains stopped and the emergency throttle cut remains independent. The preset is a starting configuration, not statistically optimal settings or profitability evidence.

To Do now introduces the machine and engine/throttle/stop workflow. Every row has a plain-language why and setup scope; optional and future boxes may stay unticked. The budget check validates effective built-in defaults plus saved overrides without writes; malformed saved state remains unknown or needs review. Missing virtual accounting is marked When needed, not a mandatory setup form. Checks remain read-only and are observations rather than authority. New navigation uses practical data-help and preserves the fixed bottom-right black/purple description panel.

First-run acceptance includes atomic fresh presets, concurrent first reads, rollback on injected storage failure, saved budget/OFF preservation, old empty-file preservation and retained-accounting/sidecar/cut suppression; the actual fixed runner prepares with defaults without manual forms or price IO. The mandatory real loopback HTTP release profile still proves passive startup creates zero files, then exercises the normal settings GET/permissions/checklist path: only the settings database is created, Terrestrial is selected, each purpose is present, optional connections are not required, an explicit OFF survives reload and trading stays stopped. ## v0.137 emergency-reset durability restoration

Reset storage failure is not a durable cut clearance. The gateway reset may already have committed and intent unlink may already have succeeded before a directory sync or later storage operation fails. Under trading-control serialization, the failure path must attempt to recreate `emergency_cut.intent` and reapply the transactional gateway emergency halt, attempting each even when the other fails, before returning the fixed 503. Keep `_halted` and the runner cut signal set, `_cut_confirmed` false and the worker disengaged. Do not report the failed reset itself as verified merely because the restorative halt later becomes recoverable. Explicit retry remains required. Preserve fail-closed startup, existing/newer cut epoch precedence, synthetic-only accounting and zero broker/funds/cloud authority. Native Windows storage/power-loss and sustained pressure remain independent gates.

## v0.138 autonomous-engine onboarding and Engage checks

The introduction and every read-only To Do checkbox explain the component's role in an autonomous trading engine. The intended production journey is install, provider-hosted account authorization/funding, risk allowance and Engage. Provider bank authorization, real funding, broker routing, learned-model execution and cloud operation remain unimplemented acceptance gates; never collect bank passwords/card details in AI settings. Current included intelligence is research/advisory; the actual autonomous executor is the fixed BTC local paper experiment. External AI is optional. Current paper use retains Start Portal and explicit Engage confirmation.

Engage first GETs a read-only local preflight, shows fixed practical blocker guidance and asks for confirmation only after a clear diagnostic. It creates no accounting, performs no repair, requests no prices and grants no running permission. The check samples current local state with a1GiB per-file diagnostic budget; this is not a journal retention cap, cross-file transaction or descriptor-bound guarantee. It cannot establish another process's ownership, clock continuity or price freshness. Actual start repeats admission; tier changes, emergency cuts and duplicate clicks during the check cannot silently initiate a session. Held linked BTC may retain exit monitoring with an OFF tier or malformed entry budget. Existing independent emergency and durable reset behavior stays in place.

The mandatory real-loopback HTTP profile checks the stopped preflight with no startup storage creation and false authority flags. Unit and actual-controller fixtures cover default-ready setup, corrupt/local sidecars and account mismatches, retained exits, rejected/promoted responses, cancellation, duplicate requests and emergency/tier changes. Native installation, rendered browser, actual market/provider/funds and independent/owner release acceptance remain separate.

## v0.139 Found Markets and checklist scope

Read FOUND_MARKETS.md before extending discovery. To Do separates current baseline prerequisites, optional improvements and future connected features. Found Markets is optional and selected by default in the page for Start Portal; legacy Portal API callers remain opt-in. It runs four candidates per 15-minute burst, up to36 requests/hour/process,256 in-memory summaries and six fresh research selections, with explicit deterministic 24-hour/30-day/180-day council screens. Start/stop cannot reset its request/cadence budget. Stop and engine admission fence publication; no background worker starts on application restart. Current-page switch preference is not persisted. GET status and checklist remain passive.

Never promote discovery scores to expected profit, calibrated predictions or execution permissions. This first connector scans public USD spot crypto, not all asset classes. It rotates a bounded sample, revisits earlier leaders, uses single-source closed bars and coarse last-day turnover, and drops stale/gapped/negative/risky evidence. The shortlist uses best eligible horizon score, not balanced diversification. Trained-model forecasts, multi-asset connectors, costs/spread/depth, holdouts, eligibility, shared-IP/cross-process budgets and sustained real-source acceptance remain open. The fixed BTC execution policy and emergency throttle remain independent.

Every new switch/button has practical black/purple help. ## v0.142 broker handoff admission and recovery headroom

The disconnected request journal now offers `begin_submission_checked`: a new marker requires the exact observed journal head and original payload hash, then runs the caller-defined evaluated-time/admission callback after acquiring the SQLite write transaction and again before commit. Reject changed terms/head, invalid/regressing returned clocks, revoked/raised admission and already-marked attempts. Only a returned commit can acknowledge the marker. Lost return/commit uncertainty never creates resend permission: reopen and recover using the original client ID. Legacy idempotent `mark_submission_uncertain` remains a diagnostic helper and must never be used as authorization to invoke a transport again. No new event schema, request payload, runtime API, broker connection, credential access, order send or financial authority is introduced. The callback is caller-defined, not an independent account/risk/funds approval; any future adapter must separately recheck authorization immediately before actual IO after marker acknowledgment.

Count two event slots per unresolved/nonterminal attempt for a next original-ID lookup begin/result pair, or one when its lookup is pending. New prepare/attempt/nonterminal observation writes must leave that aggregate headroom. Lookup admission also preserves other unresolved requests' reserves. Completion/interruption must still record their outcome using the pending slot; a timeout/nonterminal outcome can leave a future reserve deficit rather than guaranteeing indefinitely many retries. Read-only recovery reports physical slots, reserved recovery slots, ordinary slots and any deficit. Terminal observations that release their own reserve remain admissible when a physical slot exists, including older deficit histories; full journals still cannot append. Do not delete history, invent terminal states or raise caps silently.

These are bounded cooperating-version event headroom checks under SQLite serialization, not byte/disk capacity reservations, distributed ownership, provider quota/funds guarantees or broker acknowledgment/fees/position reconciliation. Old/other writers and direct database edits are outside the reserve contract. Historical replay stays readable even when its available headroom is deficient. Existing lookup-pending fences, token/account binding, cooldowns, explicit interruption and no-auto-retry remain.

Tests cover stale heads/terms, pre/post-write revocation and regressing clocks with unchanged files, real SQLite contention, concurrent same-head begins, lost return after commit, separate reservations for multiple requests, protected next-lookup completion and terminal deficit cleanup. The existing pacing release profile now requires a disposable checked-marker/reopen/original-ID lookup/terminal-reserve-release composition with all transport/routing/account/financial/release flags false. Generate and verify all16 current-source profiles before the full suite. Actual broker-paper integration, authorized account/source/fee/hold reconciliation and independent/owner1.0 gates remain open.

A retained account-level lookup review/authentication block also rejects a new checked attempt for a different prepared request before invoking its caller admission callback. The mandatory fixture requires unchanged files for that denial; no credential reset or automatic unblocking occurs.

Callbacks must be fast local checks; no network/model work while holding the write transaction. Future adapters still require separately authorized IO and a final permission check after acknowledgment.

## v0.144 durable shared-local Found Markets request budget

Public Found Markets requests consume a rolling 36-per-hour allowance in `found_market_request_budget.sqlite3` before transport. The journal is created only by an explicitly started public scan, contains timestamps only, uses bounded DELETE-mode SQLite with full synchronization, and is shared by cooperating Nexus processes using the same `NEXUS_PORTABLE_DATA`. Restart, Portal toggles, transport errors, process loss and lost responses do not refund an allowance. Serialize concurrent first use and each admission under the database writer transaction; sample and validate wall time after acquiring that transaction. Clock regression, exhaustion, malformed schema/content, unexpected WAL/shared-memory sidecars, symlinks, hardlinks, nonregular files and oversize journals must fail closed before public IO. Passive status and startup remain storage- and network-silent.

Do not describe this as provider-wide, machine-wide or shared-IP quota coordination. Other clients, data roots and hosts remain outside the journal. Keep summaries memory-only and bursts atomic; no discovered market can affect the fixed BTC paper policy, tiers, emergency stop, account state, orders or funds. Injected transports remain process-local by default unless a test explicitly supplies a budget. New budget fields are read-only diagnostics, not controls, so no new button/help treatment is required. The rough implementation estimate remains about40% (30–45%, low confidence) and0/9 major stages fully accepted: this is meaningful safety hardening, but it does not complete a major integration or acceptance stage.

## v0.145 passive broker handoff review and checked lookup clocks

`BrokerRequestJournal.submission_review(client_order_id, evaluated_at=...)` is an internal/source-level read-only diagnostic for the caller-supplied journal/account, not a new public path-selection API. Missing history leaves directories absent and reports REQUEST_NOT_PREPARED. Existing history uses bounded read-only replay and refuses account mismatch, corrupt/foreign/unsafe journals without repair. It returns the exact observed head/original payload hash, retained lookup clock floor/rate deadline, wait and capacity diagnostics with preparation, original-ID recovery, account review, pending lookup, clock, cooldown and reserve blockers. A clear result is LOCAL_MARKER_CHECKS_CLEAR only: no permission/risk/funds callback, event/byte/disk reservation, order or provider approval. All transport/trading/funding/release flags remain false. Re-evaluate the current head/terms and caller admission through begin_submission_checked; a stale clear review cannot bypass its lock-time checks. No schema/event/payload/cap changes or automatic retry/deletion occurs.

Checked original-ID lookup begin/result now reject evaluated-time regression between the pre-lock sample and locked admission, then again between the inserted event time and final callback before commit. Invalid/revoked/regressing final admission rolls back the actual inserted event; failed begin consumes no attempt, failed result preserves its pending ticket for explicit recovery. Equal times remain legal. Legacy supplied-time diagnostic methods and idempotent already-retained result acknowledgments retain their semantics; they do not create a fresh lookup/transport attempt. These checks remain local, not provider-wide quota or clock attestation.

The existing explicit Accounts offline request-recovery fixture now demonstrates a passive clear review followed by marked-attempt original-ID recovery. PORTAL rejects promoted preflight authority and clears the result on failure; missing legacy preflight evidence is unavailable. Practical button help and intercepted browser fixture cover the added explanation with the existing black/purple bottom-right help. No credentials, account storage, broker or live network is accessed by these fixtures. The mandatory pacing trial requires immutable passive review and actual post-insert clock rollback for both lookup begin/result. Review snapshots are not accepted account/funds/strategy/market evidence.

Rough progress remains about40% implementation, judgment range30–45%, low confidence,0/9 fully accepted stages. This coherent hardening batch does not close broker-paper, sustained market, native-profile, funding/live/cloud or independent/owner1.0 acceptance. Reassess estimates only on material evidence or scope change.

## v0.146 original request to retained explicit-fill review

`nexus.broker_request_activity_review.review` composes an exact BrokerRequestJournal and BrokerActivityStore for the same declared account under caller-supplied expected request/activity heads. It uses bounded read-only full replay, matches each observed original broker/client/side/status/quantity/average/time snapshot, and checks explicit REST fills against the immutable request's side/product, total/cumulative/leaves quantities and individual limit price. It reuses merged activity ambiguity, cumulative-chain, timestamp, fill-total and average-disagreement checks. All retained activity orders outside the request journal remain explicit review blockers; no match by client ID alone replaces a missing original broker observation. No execution price is inferred from cumulative averages. The report lists local agreement separately from status REVIEW_REQUIRED and persistent source/fee/hold/account blockers.

A good average cannot hide one bad execution: two unit fills at85 and113 average99, within a BUY100 aggregate limit, but the113 fill still violates that original limit. The analogous SELL98 example also requires review. Even exact local agreement never confirms complete activities, authentic provider data, fee links/timing, holds, balances, spendable funds or order authority. All source/coverage/reconciliation/ledger/routing/broker/live/funds/retry/release flags remain false. Review reads only the supplied journals; it writes no journal or ledger and accesses no credentials, network or default data path. Returned identity details are private plaintext; keep actual reports/journals outside Git. The artifact guard rejects standalone BROKER_REQUEST_ACTIVITY_REVIEW JSON even when renamed. Supplied hashes remain local consistency references, not signatures or encryption.

Both journal snapshots are replayed again after computation and any changed head/content refuses the review. Missing histories remain absent and cannot imply agreement. This is sampled cross-database rechecking, not an atomic two-database transaction, execution lock, reserved admission, source attestation or detection of tail deletion/ABA changes by external writers. Existing checked marker and lookup guards retain all current-head, cooldown, pending ticket, capacity, caller-admission and independent final-IO permission requirements. No actual broker submission path is enabled.

The Accounts request-recovery fixture now additionally constructs an original request and two explicit fills in temporary journals, verifies matching terms without writes and catches an offsetting individual-price violation. UI validates the fixture's positive checks and false authority flags, labels fee/fund verification unavailable, suppresses private failures and retains practical black/purple beginner help. Existing rendered browser fixtures exercise this explanation; local controller checks alone do not establish a rendered/native pass. The sixteen-profile suite now makes this evidence mandatory in the activity profile without replacing existing activity/source/fee assertions. Tests include BUY/SELL bounds, incorrect leaves with consistent supplied chains, stale heads, missing/foreign histories, missing original observation, changed snapshot status, incomplete fills, untracked orders, equal-time conflict, concurrent head changes and fresh-process immutable replay.

Rough implementation progress stays about40% (30–45% judgment range, low confidence),0/9 fully accepted stages. ## v0.147 explicit paced paper BTC asset fields and Windows smoke evidence

Accounts adds Read Paper BTC Rules. It requires the expected account UUID, separate confirmation and an already saved Windows paper key pair. Origin-first bounded POST `/api/execution/alpaca-paper/local-credentials/asset-rules` accepts only expected_account_id and confirm_read_only_paper; no browser path, URL, clock, symbol or transport can be supplied. Its reader performs exactly three fixed-host GETs: account, `/v2/assets/BTCUSD`, account. Stable expected account fields are required. It retains only allowlisted asset UUID, canonical BTC/USD symbol, crypto class, active/inactive status, strict tradable/fractionable booleans and positive bounded reported minimum size/trade increment/price_increment fields. Unknown/private asset attributes are discarded. Account status/permission or inactive/nontradable/fractional issues remain review reasons. No positions, order, fee or funding action is requested.

This shares the same per-account managed private pacing journal as Read Paper with Pacing, including OS ownership, pre-IO committed reservation, success cooldown, authentication/rate/error/unknown treatment, clock/permission checkpoints and final saved-pair OS readback. Bad/unknown metadata after IO leaves retained pending evidence; no retry or deletion occurs. Removing keys during IO suppresses acknowledgment. External saved-pair replacement after successful provider reads also suppresses acknowledgment without erasing already committed pacing. Other/no journals, original unpaced account reads and other hosts remain outside this coordination; it is not a provider-wide quota. Key permissions, asset entitlement, current metadata/constraint semantics, fees, funds, routing/live and release authority remain unverified/false. Never turn reported values into accepted order rounding or a tradable authorization. No cache or plan promotion is added.

The explicit control has practical bottom-right black/purple help, shares busy/generation/removal fencing, clears earlier inventory exports, validates false authority and numeric fields and provides no inventory download. Browser interception and actual-controller tests cover consent, coalescing, promoted/malformed replies, stale-result clearing and removal races. The inventory trial keeps its original12 GET checks and now additionally requires the three-GET asset treatment plus cross-reader durable cooldown; real-loopback HTTP checks the new route's denied confirmation/origin/unknown-URL paths without provider access. Standalone READ_ONLY_PAPER_ASSET_RULES reports include private account identity and are blocked by the public artifact guard even when renamed. Actual tests use injected HTTPS/vault and disposable journals only.

Primary provider references checked2026-10-18: https://docs.alpaca.markets/us/reference/get-v2-assets-symbol_or_asset_id documents paper asset lookup and legacy BTCUSD path notation; https://docs.alpaca.markets/us/docs/crypto-trading documents crypto asset fields. https://alpaca.markets/support/alpaca-crypto-coin-pair-faq describes dynamic minimums and increment semantics. These references do not constitute provider-account acceptance, a stable tick-size contract or permission to send orders; independent semantics/eligibility review remains open.
